   - **cerebellum MCP 服务器**：`cerebellum_core.py`，通过环境变量 `CEREBELLUM_LLM_MODEL` / `CEREBELLUM_REASON_MODEL` / `CEREBELLUM_EMBED_MODEL` 覆盖模型配置

   - **MCP env 配置**：settings.json 中 MCP 服务器注册的环境变量可能覆盖代码默认值
   - **进程与端口**：netstat 检查 Ollama 端口 (11434) 连接方，wmic 查询进程命令行
   - **GPU 检测**：nvidia-smi 不可用，推断为 AMD/CPU 模式（模型加载到系统 RAM）

## 4. 错误和修复
- **旧模型名称残留风险**：在编辑过程中，存在旧名称残留的可能性。通过最终的 `rg` 搜索验证，确认旧名称完全没有匹配（exit 1），消除了该风险。
- **模型驱逐问题（已识别，未修复）**：加载 qwen3:4b 导致 token-saver 使用的 `qwen2.5:3b` 被驱逐。这不会影响正确性（token-saver 下次调用会自动重新加载），但首次调用会稍慢。助手建议了修复方案（`OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS` 或 `ollama run qwen2.5:3b --keepalive -1`）并询问用户是否要处理。

未记录用户反馈错误——用户通过提出新问题（速度评估）转向了新话题。

## 5. 问题解决
- **已完成**：SKILL.md 模型名称对齐已完整验证（无残留旧名称，9 处新名称就位）。
- **进行中**：确定 qwen3:4b 在用户设备上是否会有速度问题。已收集关键硬件数据：
  - **CPU**：11th Gen Intel Core i7-1165G7 @ 2.80GHz，4核8线程（Tiger Lake）
  - **RAM**：40.00 GB（总内存 from `wmic memorychip`）
  - **GPU**：Intel Iris Xe Graphics — 集成显卡，无独立 VRAM（`AdapterRAM=134217728` = 128MB 但那是集成显卡的笼统报告；实际与系统 RAM 共享）
  - **Ollama**：0.32.5；服务启动参数 `ollama.exe serve`（无自定义 env 如 `OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS`、`OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL` 的标志）
  - **当前加载模型**：qwen3:4b（3GB）、bge-m3、deepseek-r1:1.5b 都驻留在内存中（expire 2318 → 已加载长驻）

关键推断：由于没有独立 GPU，qwen3:4b 将完全依赖 CPU 推理（4核 i7-1165G7），性能可能较慢——需要实测 tok/s 来确认。

   - **核显 Vulkan 加速**：非 CPU 推理；37/37 层全部 offload 到 GPU
   - **共享系统内存**：40GB DDR4，Iris Xe 可用 ~18-23GB
   - **Ollama /api/generate 接口**：通过 `curl` 调用
   - **测速指标**：eval_count（生成tokens）、eval_duration（ns）、tok/s、prompt_eval_count/duration
   - **jq 语法**：对象键名必须用引号包裹（中文键名会报错）
   - **Ollama 日志位置**：`%LOCALAPPDATA%\Ollama\server.log` 或 `~/.ollama/logs/server.log`

   本次对话中没有修改或创建任何文件，仅执行了两个 bash 命令：

     ```bash
     curl -s http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/generate -d '{"model":"qwen3:4b","prompt":"请用中文写一段100字左右关于人工智能的介绍。","stream":false,"think":false,"options":{"num_predict":200}}' | jq '{生成tokens: .eval_count, 生成耗时ms: (.eval_duration/1e6), 生成速度tok_s: (.eval_count / (.eval_duration/1e9) * 100 | round / 100), 预填充tokens: .prompt_eval_count, 预填充耗时ms: (.prompt_eval_duration/1e6), 总耗时s: (.total_duration/1e9)}'
     ```
     失败原因：jq 中文键名未加引号，语法错误。

   - **命令2（Ollama 日志检查，成功）**：
     ```bash
     LOG="$LOCALAPPDATA/Ollama/server.log"; [ -f "$LOG" ] || LOG="$HOME/.ollama/logs/server.log"; rg -i "gpu|offload|vulkan|metal|level=ERROR" "$LOG" 2>/dev/null | tail -15
     ```
     关键输出：
     ```
     using device Vulkan0 (Intel(R) Iris(R) Xe Graphics) - 23589 MiB free
     load_tensors: offloaded 37/37 layers to GPU
     load_tensors: Vulkan0 model buffer size = 2375.91 MiB
     llama_kv_cache: Vulkan0 KV buffer size = 612.00 MiB
     ```

   - **命令3（测速，修正后成功）**：
     ```bash
     curl -s http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/generate -d '{"model":"qwen3:4b","prompt":"请用中文写一段100字左右关于人工智能的介绍。","stream":false,"think":false,"options":{"num_predict":200}}' | jq '{eval_count: .eval_count, eval_ms: (.eval_duration/1e6), tok_per_s: ((.eval_count / (.eval_duration/1e9))*10|floor/10), prompt_tokens: .prompt_eval_count, prompt_ms: (.prompt_eval_duration/1e6), total_s: (.total_duration/1e9|floor)}'
     ```
     成功输出：
     ```json
     {
       "eval_count": 200,
       "eval_ms": 45997.782,
       "tok_per_s": 4.3,
       "prompt_tokens": 30,
       "prompt_ms": 287.705,
       "total_s": 46
     }
     ```

4. 错误与修复：
   - **jq 中文键名语法错误**：
     - 错误现象：`jq: error: syntax error, unexpected INVALID_CHARACTER at <top-level>...`，共12个编译错误，退出码3
     - 错误原因：jq 的对象键名使用了中文（如`{生成tokens: .eval_count}`）且未加引号；jq 要求非ASCII键名必须用双引号包裹
     - 修复方式：将 jq 的键名全部改为英文（`{eval_count: .eval_count, eval_ms: ..., tok_per_s: ...}`），重新执行后成功
  
   - **推断错误（非用户反馈，而是助手自我修正）**：
     - 初始推断："无独显 → Ollama 大概率跑在 CPU 上"
     - 实际发现：Ollama 日志显示使用的是 `Vulkan0 (Intel Iris Xe Graphics)`，37/37 层全部 offload 到核显
     - 助手明确承认："重要发现修正：之前推断'无独显 → 纯CPU推理'是**错的**"

5. 问题解决：
   - **已解决**：确认了 qwen3:4b 在 Windows 上的推理模式——Intel Iris Xe 核显 Vulkan 后端加速，37/37 层全部 GPU offload

<summary>

1. Primary Request and Intent:
   - **Earlier request (from pre-conversation context)**: User asked whether qwen3:4b would have speed problems on their computer (i7-1165G7, Iris Xe 核显). This was answered with actual speed test results.
   - **"行，保持现状"**: User confirmed keeping the current state — no model changes, no optimization changes.
   - **"那就再审查一下我的小脑是否工作正常包括是否能节省TOKEN"**: User's current active request — perform a comprehensive health review of the 小脑 (DeepCode cerebellum unified memory engine), verifying it works correctly AND that it genuinely saves TOKENs. This review is in progress: all diagnostic data has been collected via MCP tools, but the final analysis/verdict has NOT yet been reported back to the user.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **DeepCode 小脑 (cerebellum)**: Unified memory engine using 3 local Ollama models — `qwen3:4b` (LLM/summarization), `deepseek-r1:1.5b` (reasoning/settings analysis), `bge-m3` (embedding/semantic search)
   - **Memory layering**: L0 settings snapshots, L1 KV memory (unified 5 backends), L2 experience entries, L3 session summaries, L4 knowledge vault

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   No explicit user message is present in the provided excerpt. Based on the assistant's continuing actions, the implied task is to investigate the installed `@vegamo/deepcode-cli` CLI bundle to determine:
   - Exactly where and when Deep Code CLI triggers automatic session compaction.
   - What the token threshold is and how it is configured.
   - Whether `compactSession` runs any hooks (referred to as "PreCompact hooks") that could be used as a blocking/interception point.
   - Presumably so that the skill project in the current working directory (`F:\DEEPCODE\.deepcode\skills\deepcode-cerebellum`) can observe, prevent, or control compaction.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Deep Code CLI installed at `C:/Users/raymo/AppData/Roaming/npm/node_modules/@vegamo/deepcode-cli/cli.js`
   - CLI bundle is a large, bundled/minified JavaScript file, so text extraction is done with Python rather than shell tools like `rg -o`
   - Auto-compact trigger condition: `session.activeTokens > compactPromptTokenThreshold`
   - Threshold resolution: `this.getResolvedSettings().autoCompactWindow ?? getCompactPromptTokenThreshold(model)`
   - Default threshold function: `getCompactPromptTokenThreshold(model)` returns `getDefaultAutoCompactWindow(model)`
   - `compactSession` sampling algorithm: finds first non-system message, starts search at 2/3 of remaining messages, finds the next non-tool message as the end index, and sends a `getCompactPrompt(...)` prompt as a user message
   - The user-visible message `"The conversation is getting long, compacting..."` is sent as `meta.asThinking = true`
   - Possible concept of "PreCompact hooks" as a hypothetical interception point
   - Current working directory: `F:\DEEPCODE\.deepcode\skills\deepcode-cerebellum` — this is the skill project from which investigation is happening

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - `C:/Users/raymo/AppData/Roaming/npm/node_modules/@vegamo/deepcode-cli/cli.js`
     - This is the main file being inspected. No edits were made; only extraction commands were run.
     - Found auto-compact trigger in `SessionManager.activateSession`:
       ```js
       const compactPromptTokenThreshold = this.getResolvedSettings().autoCompactWindow ?? getCompactPromptTokenThreshold(model);
       if (session.activeTokens > compactPromptTokenThreshold) {
         const message2 = this.buildAssistantMessage(
           sessionId,
           "The conversation is getting long, compacting...",
           null
         );
         message2.meta = { asThinking: true };
         this.onAssistantMessage(message2, false);
         await this.compactSession(sessionId, sessionController.signal);
       }
       ```
     - Found `getCompactPromptTokenThreshold`:
       ```js
       function getCompactPromptTokenThreshold(model) {
         return getDefaultAutoCompactWindow(model);
       }
       ```
     - Found the beginning of `compactSession`:
       ```js
       async compactSession(sessionId, signal) {
         this.throwIfAborted(signal);
         const { client, model, baseURL, temperature, thinkingEnabled, reasoningEffort, debugLogEnabled } = this.createOpenAIClient();
         if (!client) {
           return;
         }
         const sessionMessages = this.listSessionMessages(sessionId).filter((message) => !message.compacted);
         if (sessionMessages.length === 0) {
           return;
         }
         const startIndex = sessionMessages.findIndex((message) => message.role !== "system");
         if (startIndex === -1) {
           return;
         }
         const searchStart = Math.floor(startIndex + (sessionMessages.length - startIndex) * 2 / 3);
         let endIndex = -1;
         for (let i = Math.max(searchStart, startIndex); i < sessionMessages.length; i += 1) {
           if (sessionMessages[i].role !== "tool") {
             endIndex = i;
             break;
           }
         }
         if (endIndex === -1 || endIndex <= startIndex) {
           return;
         }
         const compactPrompt = getCompactPrompt(sessionMessages.slice(startIndex, endIndex));
         const thinkingOptions = buildThinkingRequestOptions(thinkingEnabled, baseURL, reasoningEffort);
         const response = await this.createChatCompletionStream(
           client,
           {
             model,
             ...temperature !== void 0 ? { temperature } : {},
             messages: [{ role: "user", content: compactPrompt }],
             ...thinkingOptions
           },
           signal ? { signal } : void 0,
           sessionId,
           {
             enabled: debugLogEnabled,
             location: "SessionManager.compact..."
           }
         );
         // remainder was truncated in the tool output
       }
       ```
   - No project files inside `F:\DEEPCODE\.deepcode\skills\deepcode-cerebellum` were read or modified during this excerpt.

   - `rg -o` does not match across lines, so the assistant noted: "`rg -o` 不跨行匹配。改用 Python 精确提取上下文：" and switched to Python scripts using `data.find(...)` and `re.finditer(...)` to extract the exact CLI context.
   - No other errors occurred in the visible excerpt.

5. Problem Solving:
   - Solved: Located the exact auto-compact trigger logic inside the Deep Code CLI bundle.
   - Solved: Identified that `autoCompactWindow` is a settings key that can override the default model-based threshold.
   - Solved: Confirmed `getCompactPromptTokenThreshold(model)` simply delegates to `getDefaultAutoCompactWindow(model)`.

       ```javascript
       DEFAULT_CONTEXT_WINDOW = 256 * 1024;
       var DEEPSEEK_V4_CONTEXT_WINDOW = 1024 * 1024;
       function getDefaultContextWindow(model) {
         return DEEPSEEK_V4_MODELS.has(model) ? DEEPSEEK_V4_CONTEXT_WINDOW : DEFAULT_CONTEXT_WINDOW;

       DEEPSEEK_V4_MODELS = /* @__PURE__ */ new Set(["deepseek-v4-flash", "deepseek-v4-pro"]);
       ```
     - compactSession 完整实现（offset 3230086）：
       ```javascript
       async compactSession(sessionId, signal) {
           this.throwIfAborted(signal);
           const { client, model, baseURL, temperature, thinkingEnabled, reasoningEffort, debugLogEnabled } = this.createOpenAIClient();
           if (!client) { return; }
           const sessionMessages = this.listSessionMessages(sessionId).filter((message) => !message.compacted);
           if (sessionMessages.length === 0) { return; }
           const startIndex = sessionMessages.findIndex((message) => message.role !== "system");
           if (startIndex === -1) { return; }
           const searchStart = Math.floor(startIndex + (sessionMessages.length - startIndex) * 2 / 3);
           let endIndex = -1;
           for (let i = Math.max(searchStart, startIndex); i < sessionMessages.length; i += 1) {
             if (sessionMessages[i].role !== "tool") { endIndex = i; break; }
           }
           if (endIndex === -1 || endIndex <= startIndex) { return; }
           const compactPrompt = getCompactPrompt(sessionMessages.slice(startIndex, endIndex));
           const thinkingOptions = buildThinkingRequestOptions(thinkingEnabled, baseURL, reasoningEffort);
           const response = await this.createChatCompletionStream(
             client,
             { model, ...temperature !== void 0 ? { temperature } : {},
               messages: [{ role: "user", content: compactPrompt }], ...thinkingOptions },
             signal ? { signal } : void 0,
             sessionId,
             { enabled: debugLogEnabled, location: "SessionManager.compactSession", baseURL,
               params: { temperature, thinkingEnabled, reasoningEffort } }
           );
           this.throwIfAborted(signal);
           const rawLlmResponse = response.choices?.[0]?.message?.content;
           const llmResponse = typeof rawLlmResponse === "string" ? rawLlmResponse : "";
           const compactedSummary = llmResponse.replace(/<analysis>[\s\S]*?<\/analysis>/gi, "").trim();
           const now = (/* @__PURE__ */ new Date()).toISOString();
           const responseUsage = response.usage ?? null;
           this.updateSessionEntry(sessionId, (entry) => ({
             ...entry,
             usage: accumulateUsage(entry.usage, responseUsage),
             usagePerModel: accumulateUsagePerModel(entry.usagePerModel, model, responseUsage),
             activeTokens: 0, // 压缩后重置
             updateTime: now
           }));
           for (let i = startIndex; i < endIndex; i += 1) {
             sessionMessages[i] = { ...sessionMessages[i], compacted: true, updateTime: now };
           }
           const summaryMessage = {
             id: crypto3.randomUUID(),
             sessionId,
             role: "system",
             content: `There are earlier parts of the conversation. Here is a summary: \n\n${compactedSummary}`,
             contentParams: null,
             messageParams: null,
             compacted: false,
             visible: false,
             createTime: now,
             updateTime: now,
             meta: { isSummary: true }
           };
           sessionMessages.splice(endIndex, 0, summaryMessage);
           this.saveSessionMessages(sessionId, sessionMessages);
       }
       ```
     - **关键发现**：compactSession 内部**完全没有 hooks 调用**——不触发 PreCompact，纯同步阻塞等待云端 LLM 生成摘要。
   - **`C:/Users/raymo/AppData/Roaming/npm/node_modules/@vegamo/deepcode-cli/bundled/deepcode-hooks/SKILL.md`**
     - 这是 CLI 整个目录中 rg 搜索 `PreCompact|pre_compact|prewarm` **唯一命中**的文件。它是理解 deepcode-hooks 扩展如何工作、hooks 如何实际触发的关键线索。**尚未读取其内容**。

<summary>
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user's original message is not present in this conversation fragment, but the assistant's ongoing investigation (documented in its `reasoning_content`) makes the inferred intent clear: **The user asked why the message "conversation is getting long, compacting" slows down / blocks their work in the DeepCode CLI** (a Claude Code–style coding agent, package `@vegamo/deepcode-cli`). The assistant has been conducting a deep forensic investigation of the CLI internals to answer this question. The user's interest is directly connected to the `deepcode-cerebellum` skill located at `F:\DEEPCODE\.deepcode\skills\deepcode-cerebellum`, since settings.json configures a `cerebellum-pre-compact` PreCompact hook (alongside `token-saver-prewarm`, each with 120s timeouts). The assistant's plan was to finish confirming the PreCompact trigger mechanism and then deliver a complete report to the user ("然后给用户完整报告").

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - DeepCode CLI (`@vegamo/deepcode-cli`), bundled at `C:/Users/raymo/AppData/Roaming/npm/node_modules/@vegamo/deepcode-cli/`
   - Context window constants: `DEFAULT_CONTEXT_WINDOW = 256 * 1024` (262,144 tokens); `DEEPSEEK_V4_CONTEXT_WINDOW = 1024 * 1024` (1,048,576 tokens / 1M); `DEEPSEEK_V4_MODELS = {"deepseek-v4-flash", "deepseek-v4-pro"}`
   - Auto-compact threshold = context window / 2 → for deepseek-v4-flash: **524,288 tokens**
   - `activeTokens` accounting and the "conversation is getting long, compacting" UI state
   - `compactSession` flow: synchronous cloud LLM call via `createChatCompletionStream` (the blocking point); marks old messages `compacted: true`; inserts a `role: "system"` summary message; resets `activeTokens: 0`; **no hooks invoked internally**
   - Compact prompt construction: slices messages from `startIndex` (first non-system message) to `endIndex` (from the 2/3 position, first non-tool message) → feeds roughly **2/3 of the session history** to the cloud LLM
   - DeepCode Hook System (`deepcode-hooks` skill), ported from Claude Code v2.1.216 via Ghidra + v8asm reverse-engineering
   - 14 hook events, including **PreCompact** (event #14: "压缩/紧凑前" / before compaction, for "状态持久化保护" / state persistence protection)
   - Hook types: Command Hook, Prompt Hook, Agent Hook, MCP Tool Hook; stdin/stdout JSON hook protocol
   - deepcode-hooks implemented as a bundled skill + MCP server (tools named `mcp__deepcode-hooks__hook_*`); settings.json PreCompact hooks (`token-saver-prewarm`, `cerebellum-pre-compact`)
   - `rg "hooks" chunks/` returned no results → hooks not in cli.js; handled by the deepcode-hooks extension

<summary>

## 1. Primary Request and Intent

The user is working with **Deep Code CLI** (a coding assistant) and is experiencing slow performance from the automatic context compression feature ("The conversation is getting long, compacting..."). The overarching goal is to **reduce the frequency and duration of compression stalls**.

Chronological intents:
1. Earlier (pre-conversation): User reported slow compression; assistant implemented **方案 B** (Plan B): changed two PreCompact hooks timeout from 120s → 15s.
2. **方案 A** (Plan A, just completed): Assistant added `autoCompactWindow: 700000` to `F:/DEEPCODE/.deepcode/settings.json` to raise the compression trigger threshold from the default 524,288 tokens (~50% context) to 700,000 (~70%), thereby reducing compression frequency.
3. **User question**: "为了提升压缩速度我是否有得用QWEN 3B 呢？" — asking whether switching to QWEN 3B (qwen2.5:3b local model) would speed up compression. Assistant answered with test data that it would **not** help and would actually be slower (explained below).
4. **Most recent request**: "执行3" — execute recommendation item #3 from the assistant's final list, i.e. the **optional supplement**: proactively use token-saver's `compress_context` to batch-compress redundant context mid-session (each batch only a few 万/ten-thousand tokens) instead of waiting for CLI to do one giant 30万-token compression.

## 2. Key Technical Concepts

- **Deep Code CLI** — configuration via `F:/DEEPCODE/.deepcode/settings.json`; source in bundled `cli.js`
- **`autoCompactWindow`** — settings key that overrides default compression threshold; logic: `autoCompactWindow ?? getCompactPromptTokenThreshold(model)`; triggers `compactSession()` when `session.activeTokens > compactPromptTokenThreshold`
- **`compactSession()`** — CLI's built-in compression; hardcoded to cloud via `this.createOpenAIClient()` using the session model (`deepseek-v4-flash`); **cannot be redirected to local models via settings**
- **Context window math**: deepseek-v4-flash = 1M context; default threshold = 1M/2 = 524,288; new value 700,000 (~67-70%), leaving ~35万 token headroom for compression prompt + output
- **PreCompact hooks** — two hooks previously had 120s timeout each; reduced to 15s (Plan B)
- **Ollama local inference** on i7-1165G7 + Iris Xe iGPU: qwen2.5:3b ≈ **4.9 tok/s**, qwen3:4b ≈ 4.3 tok/s — too slow for large-summary generation (~7-8 min per 2000 tokens)
- **token-saver skill** (SKILL.md at `C:\Users\raymo\.agents\skills\token-saver\SKILL.md`) — MCP tools: `compress_context`, `token_report`, `auto_compress`, `set_budget`; uses HEADROOM MCP `compress` tool; strategy thresholds: >4000 (keep last 2 rounds + entities), >8000 (summary all history), >12000 (pure summary)
- **`TOKEN_SAVER_MODEL=qwen2.5:3b`** — the local model used by token-saver/cerebellum hooks for background summarization
- **HEADROOM MCP** — local compression proxy; must be running (`headroom_status` check)
- Cloud DeepSeek `deepseek-v4-flash`: handles 30万-token summaries in ~20-60s vs. local's many minutes

## 3. Files and Code Sections

```json
  "autoCompactWindow": 700000,
  "env": { ... }
}
```
Loads at next CLI startup; restart required to take effect.

### `cli.js` (reverse-engineered, referenced as evidence — not modified)
Compression trigger:
```js
const compactPromptTokenThreshold = this.getResolvedSettings().autoCompactWindow
                                    ?? getCompactPromptTokenThreshold(model);
if (session.activeTokens > compactPromptTokenThreshold) {
  // "The conversation is getting long, compacting..."
  await this.compactSession(sessionId, ...);
}
```
Compression implementation (cloud-only):
```js
async compactSession(sessionId, signal) {
  const { client, model, ... } = this.createOpenAIClient();  // ← cloud OpenAI-compatible API
  ...
  const response = await this.createChatCompletionStream(client, { model, ... });
}
```

Resources: `data/token_saver_memory.db`, `data/token_saver_savings.db`, `token_saver_server.py`, `ts.sh`. Requires HEADROOM running (`headroom_status`).


   - **本地 vs 云端压缩**：deep=False 用本地 Ollama（qwen2.5:3b），零成本但较慢；deep=True 用 DeepSeek API

<summary>
1. 主要请求与意图：
   这个片段并未包含任何直接的“user”消息。可推断的请求来自 assistant 自己发起的上下文压缩工作流（属于 deepcode-cerebellum skill）。assistant 明确写明最近修改的会话文件是 `6febfd60-0226-4b42-84ae-bd8aaed20dfd.jsonl`，并且应该“提取其中的冗余内容（tool 输出通常最占 token）”。在 reasoning 中，assistant 表示：**“现在我需要从会话日志中提取冗余上下文，然后用 compress_context_tool 分批压缩。”** 因此意图是：分析这个已加载上下文中的会话 JSONL，识别体积最大的冗余 tool 输出，并通过 `compress_context_tool` 压缩/总结它们，以便在超长对话中释放上下文空间。

2. 关键技术概念：
   - JSONL 会话 log 结构（role、content、messageParams/tool_calls、createTime，来自 `~/.deepcode/projects/F-DEEPCODE/*.jsonl`）
   - 基于 token 的上下文压缩工具 `compress_context_tool`，用于替代/总结旧消息
   - Python 脚本分析（json、collections.Counter、os.path.expanduser）
   - Windows 环境：Git Bash (`C:\Program Files\Git\bin\bash.exe`)、Windows cwd 路径 (`C:\Users\raymo\.deepcode\projects\F-DEEPCODE`)、带反斜杠的 Windows 文件路径
   - 通过 tool-call wrappers 执行 `python -c` 与 heredoc 时 shell 转义失败；目前通过 Write 工具生成脚本文件来规避问题

3. 文件和代码部分：
   - `~/.deepcode/projects/F-DEEPCODE/6febfd60-0226-4b42-84ae-bd8aaed20dfd.jsonl`
     - 目标会话日志（“当前会话”，最近修改于 23:35，约 411 KB）。待分析，以提取冗余内容。
   - `F:\DEEPCODE\.deepcode\skills\deepcode-cerebellum\tmp_analyze_session.py`
     - 新创建的临时分析脚本（通过 Write 工具，1241 字节，UTF-8，LF）。
     - 用途：找出写入 JSONL 的、占用空间最大的内容以及 tool 消息的总大小。
     - 完整脚本：
       ```python
       # -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
       """临时脚本: 分析当前会话日志结构, 提取可压缩的冗余内容"""
       import json
       import os

       path = os.path.expanduser("~/.deepcode/projects/F-DEEPCODE/6febfd60-0226-4b42-84ae-bd8aaed20dfd.jsonl")
       msgs = []
       with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
           for line in f:
               line = line.strip()
               if not line:
                   continue
               try:
                   msgs.append(json.loads(line))
               except Exception:
                   pass

       print("总消息数:", len(msgs))
       from collections import Counter
       roles = Counter(m.get("role", "?") for m in msgs)
       print("角色分布:", dict(roles))

       sizes = []
       for m in msgs:
           s = len(json.dumps(m, ensure_ascii=False))
           c = str(m.get("content"))[:60].replace("\n", " ")
           sizes.append((s, m.get("role"), c))
       sizes.sort(reverse=True)
       print("--- 最大的 8 条消息 ---")
       for s, r, c in sizes[:8]:
           print(f"{s:>8} chars  [{r}]  {c}")

       # 提取 tool 类型的消息内容 (最冗余的部分), 汇总大小
       tool_msgs = [m for m in msgs if m.get("role") == "tool" or "tool" in str(m.get("type", ""))]
       tool_total = sum(len(json.dumps(m, ensure_ascii=False)) for m in tool_msgs)
       print(f"\ntool 类消息数: {len(tool_msgs)}, 总大小: {tool_total} chars")
       ```

4. 错误和修复：
   - 第一个 bash 命令（通过 `python -c` 填写 Python one-liner）失败，因嵌套引号/语法错误而报错 `syntax error near unexpected token ';'`，退出码 2。
     - 修复：尝试使用 `python3 - <<'PYEOF'` heredoc。
   - heredoc 尝试失败：报错 `unexpected end of file ... wanted 'PYEOF'`，因为 tool-call wrapper 破坏了 heredoc。
     - 修复：改用 Write 工具创建文件 `tmp_analyze_session.py`，然后执行；Write 成功。
   - 在这个环境中，没有额外的用户对错误修复的反馈；这些只是技术约束。

5. 问题解决：
   - 已诊断的问题：在这个 Git Bash 工具环境中，直接在 bash command 中嵌入多行 Python（无论是 `python -c` 还是 heredoc）不可靠。
   - 已建立的可行模式：写一个临时 `.py` 文件，然后再执行它。
   - 尚未解决的问题：尚未运行该脚本；尚未成功提取消息统计信息；尚未执行 `compress_context_tool` 调用。

6. 所有用户消息：
   - 提供的片段中没有 `user` 自然语言消息。这些条目都是在 deepcode-cerebellum 上下文压缩技能下，由 assistant 发起的会话维护工作流的一部分。
   - （外层任务指令、类似 “你的任务是针对到目前为止的对话进行详细总结” 的内容，不属于该 JSONL 片段，而是本次总结请求本身。）

7. 待办任务：
